🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：equivariant graph neural network、graph neural networks、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势（MLIP）通过访问前所未有的长度和时间尺度正在改变原子模拟。虽然预训练的等变图神经网络在广泛的化学空间中实现了近平衡特性的鲁棒零样本性能，但它们向复杂材料动力学的转化仍然受到分布外反应状态、表示偏差和计算缩放限制的根本挑战。在这篇综述中，我们研究了物理驱动的专业化如何将 MLIP 的适用性扩展到复杂的动力系统。我们系统地评估了 MLIP 设计中的结构权衡：高度紧张配置的欠采样、高阶消息传递架构的令人望而却步的计算开销，以及开放和场耦合系统非局部交互的必要性。通过四个要求严格的应用环境——电气化接口、成分波动的开放系统、多相演化和大规模断裂——我们建立了一个可观测特定验证的框架。我们强调通用基础模型和特定任务潜力的互补作用，强调有针对性的适应必须根据预期的可观察结果进行严格的基准测试。最后，我们提出了开发物理一致、硬件感知力场的路线图，将电子结构精度与宏观材料现象无缝连接。

English Abstract

Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are transforming atomistic simulations by accessing unprecedented length and time scales. While pretrained equivariant graph neural networks achieve robust zero-shot performance for near-equilibrium properties across broad chemical spaces, their translation to complex materials dynamics remains fundamentally challenged by out-of-distribution reactive states, representation biases, and computational scaling limits. In this Review, we examine how physics-driven specialization extends the applicability of MLIPs to complex dynamical systems. We systematically evaluate the structural trade-offs in MLIP design: the undersampling of highly strained configurations, the prohibitive computational overhead of high-order message-passing architectures, and the necessity of nonlocal interactions for open and field-coupled systems. Through four demanding application contexts - electrified interfaces, compositionally fluctuating open systems, multiphase evolution, and large-scale fracture - we establish a framework for observable-specific validation. Highlighting the complementary roles of universal foundation models and task-specific potentials, we emphasize that targeted adaptations must be rigorously benchmarked against intended observables. We conclude with a roadmap for developing physically consistent, hardware-aware force fields that seamlessly connect electronic-structure accuracy to macroscopic materials phenomena.

评估用于评估晶体学开放数据库准确性的基础机器学习潜力的可靠性#

原题：Assessing the Reliability of Foundational Machine Learning Potentials for Evaluating the Veracity of the Crystallography Open Database

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：晶体学开放数据库 (COD) 包含超过 50 万个通过实验确定的晶体结构。晶体学数据的验证在三个层次上进行，其中第三个层次评估结构的物理合理性。这一水平通常依赖于经验启发法或专家判断，考虑到晶体学数据库的规模和新结构沉积的速度，这些判断速度缓慢且容易出错。在这里，我们评估通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）是否可以自动执行此级别的验证。在 522 086 个 COD 条目中，317 457 个（60.8%）通过了数据质量过滤器，去除了无序结…

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：machine learning potential、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

晶体学开放数据库 (COD) 包含超过 50 万个通过实验确定的晶体结构。晶体学数据的验证在三个层次上进行，其中第三个层次评估结构的物理合理性。这一水平通常依赖于经验启发法或专家判断，考虑到晶体学数据库的规模和新结构沉积的速度，这些判断速度缓慢且容易出错。在这里，我们评估通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）是否可以自动执行此级别的验证。在 522 086 个 COD 条目中，317 457 个（60.8%）通过了数据质量过滤器，去除了无序结构、配方不匹配和未建模溶剂。这些结构通过 M3GNet 得到松弛，并且 314 153 (99.0%) 收敛，而细胞体积没有大的变化。对于大多数条目来说，宽松的交易量和存入的交易量几乎相同。因此，收敛和体积变化为识别不能确认沉积结构的弛豫提供了简单的标准。对 327 个结构的手动检查表明，没有单一描述符阈值（能量、最大力或原子位移）可以区分有效结构和无效结构。 M3GNet、CHGNet、PET-MAD 和 PET-OAM 在 524 个选定结构上的比较揭示了模型特定的失败：M3GNet 扭曲了环戊二烯基和其他 π 配体，M3GNet 和 CHGNet 都扭曲了噻吩和噻唑环，而 PET 模型保留了这些基序。所有模型都检测到缺失的氢原子，至少有一些检测到假氢、错误分配的原子类型以及两个先前未报告的坐标错误。没有一个模型能够检测到所有错误类型。因此，MLIP 松弛是一种有用但依赖于模型的筛选工具，并且组合多个模型优于依赖任何单个模型。

English Abstract

The Crystallography Open Database (COD) contains over half a million experimentally determined crystal structures. Validation of crystallographic data proceeds at three hierarchical levels, the third of which assesses the physical plausibility of structures. This level has typically relied on empirical heuristics or expert judgment, which are slow and error-prone given the size of crystallographic databases and the rate at which new structures are deposited. Here we assess whether universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) can automate this level of validation. Of the 522 086 COD entries, 317 457 (60.8%) passed a data-quality filter removing disordered structures, formula mismatches and unmodelled solvent. These structures were relaxed with M3GNet, and 314 153 (99.0%) converged without a large change in cell volume. For most entries the relaxed and deposited volumes are nearly identical. Convergence and volume change thus provide simple criteria for identifying relaxations that do not confirm the deposited structure. A manual inspection of 327 structures showed that no single descriptor threshold (energy, largest force, or atomic displacement) separates valid from invalid structures. A comparison of M3GNet, CHGNet, PET-MAD and PET-OAM on 524 selected structures revealed model-specific failures: M3GNet distorts cyclopentadienyl and other π ligands, and both M3GNet and CHGNet distort thiophene and thiazole rings, whereas the PET models preserve these motifs. All models detected missing hydrogen atoms, and at least some detected spurious hydrogens, incorrectly assigned atom types, and two previously unreported coordinate errors. No single model detected all error types. MLIP relaxation is therefore a useful but model-dependent screening tool, and combining several models is preferable to relying on any single one.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 可以实现大规模原子模拟，但在描述电荷排序强烈影响热力学稳定性的混合价材料方面仍然面临挑战。在这里，我们研究了构型电荷排序在电池正极材料 NaFePO4 的 MLIP 结构优化中的作用。我们表明，传统的 MLIP 无法重现中间 Na 浓度的正确稳定性，因为结构优化会导致不正确的 Fe2+/Fe3+ 电荷分配，从而导致不正确的能量排序和凸包预测。对结构优化过程中 Fe2+/Fe3+ 电荷分配的分析表明，MLIP 无法捕获构型电荷排序，从而导致能量误差，从而难以将实验观察到的相识别为真正的基态结构。为了解决这个限制，我们引入了一种方法，通过在训练期间区分 Fe2+ 和 Fe3+ 环境，将电荷态信息直接编码到 MLIP 表示中。使用这种表示形式重新训练 CHGNet、MACE 和 MACELES 可以实现精确的结构优化、电荷排序的正确识别，并提高与密度泛函理论凸包的一致性。我们的结果表明，将构型电荷排序纳入 MLIP 表示对于电荷无序材料建模至关重要。除了恢复正确的基态之外，这种编码还允许在优化过程中施加并保持固定的特定电荷顺序，这是不受约束的 DFT 难以保证的能力，我们证明它可以推广到四种聚阴离子框架和四种过渡金属离子（Fe、Mn、Co、Ni），建立了一种广泛适用的混合价过渡金属系统建模策略。

English Abstract

Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable large-scale atomistic simulations but remain challenged in describing mixed-valence materials where charge ordering strongly influences thermodynamic stability. Here we investigate the role of configurational charge ordering in MLIP structural optimization of the battery cathode material NaFePO4. We show that conventional MLIPs fail to reproduce the correct stability of intermediate Na concentrations because structural optimization leads to incorrect Fe2+/Fe3+ charge assignments, resulting in incorrect energy ordering and convex-hull predictions. Analysis of the Fe2+/Fe3+ charge assignment during structural optimization reveals that MLIPs are unable to capture the configurational charge ordering, leading to energy errors that make it difficult to identify the experimentally observed phase as the true ground-state structure. To address this limitation, we introduce an approach that encodes charge-state information directly into the MLIP representation by distinguishing between Fe2+ and Fe3+ environments during training. Retraining CHGNet, MACE and MACELES with this representation enables accurate structural optimization, correct identification of charge ordering, and improved agreement with density functional theory convex hulls. Our results demonstrate that incorporating configurational charge ordering into the MLIP representation is essential for modeling charge-disordered materials. Beyond recovering the correct ground state, this encoding allows a specific charge ordering to be imposed and held fixed during optimization, a capability unconstrained DFT struggles to guarantee, and we show that it generalizes across four polyanion frameworks and four transition-metal ions (Fe, Mn, Co, Ni), establishing a broadly applicable strategy for modeling mixed-valence transition-metal systems.

中文摘要

基于多项式旋转不变量的多项式机器学习势（MLP）已针对各种系统进行了系统开发，并应用于有效预测晶体结构。在这项研究中，我们提出了一种基于多项式 MLP 的方法，用于枚举高压条件下的晶体结构并评估其在有限温度下的相稳定性。所提出的方法包括在广泛的压力范围内构建具有高预测精度的多项式 MLP，对包含指定数量原子的结构进行可靠的全局结构搜索，并执行详尽的自洽声子计算以减少误差。我们通过在高达 100 GPa 的压力和高达 1000 K 的温度下检查元素硅来证明这种方法的有效性，揭示了这些条件下的稳定相。本研究建立的框架为预测高压和有限温度条件下的晶体结构和相稳定性提供了强大的策略。

English Abstract

Polynomial machine learning potentials (MLPs) based on polynomial rotational invariants have been systematically developed for various systems and applied to efficiently predict crystal structures. In this study, we propose a methodology founded on polynomial MLPs to enumerate crystal structures under high-pressure conditions and evaluate their phase stability at finite temperatures. The proposed approach involves constructing polynomial MLPs with high predictive accuracy across a broad range of pressures, conducting reliable global structure searches for structures containing up to the specified number of atoms, and performing exhaustive self-consistent phonon calculations with reduced errors. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this approach by examining elemental silicon at pressures up to 100 GPa and temperatures up to 1000 K, revealing stable phases across these conditions. The framework established in this study offers a powerful strategy for predicting crystal structures and phase stability under high-pressure and finite-temperature conditions.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

氮化铝（AlN）是一种重要的热管理材料，其高晶格热导率受到氧杂质的强烈抑制。我们使用预先训练的通用机器学习原子间势 (MLIP)、分子动力学 (MD) 和声子玻尔兹曼输运计算来研究与氧相关的缺陷引起的声子散射。几个预训练的 MLIP 以声子色散和原始热导率的密度泛函理论为基准。为了平衡精度和计算速度，我们使用紧凑 SevenNet-Nano 模型的微调版本进行 MD 模拟。蒙特卡罗退火支持结合 VAₗ(O_N)₃ 配合物的形成，其散射与其孤立成分的散射不同。从过量光谱能量密度 (SED) 线宽中提取的缺陷散射率与低氧含量下的谐波 T 矩阵预测相当一致，支持独立散射体近似。将这些速率与玻色-爱因斯坦统计数据结合到迭代玻尔兹曼传输方程中，可以得到与实验值相当的热导率，并捕获观察到的热导率随氧含量的变化。然而，在高浓度下，散射率偏离与氧含量的线性比例，这表明独立缺陷和原始声子描述的局限性。这些结果证明了一种使用预训练的 MLIP 来量化缺陷限制热传输的有效方法，并提供了对稀缺陷近似之外的杂质影响的深入了解。

English Abstract

Aluminum nitride (AlN) is an important thermal management material whose high lattice thermal conductivity is strongly suppressed by oxygen impurities. We investigate phonon scattering by oxygen-related defects using pretrained universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs), molecular dynamics (MD), and phonon Boltzmann transport calculations. Several pretrained MLIPs are benchmarked against density functional theory for phonon dispersions and pristine thermal conductivity. To balance accuracy and computational speed, we use a fine-tuned version of the compact SevenNet-Nano model for MD simulations. Monte Carlo annealing supports the formation of bound VAₗ(O_N)₃ complexes, whose scattering differs from that of their isolated constituents. Defect scattering rates extracted from excess spectral energy density (SED) linewidths agree reasonably with harmonic T-matrix predictions at low oxygen contents, supporting the independent-scatterer approximation. Incorporating these rates into an iterative Boltzmann transport equation with Bose--Einstein statistics yields thermal conductivities comparable to experimental values and captures their observed decrease with oxygen content. At high concentrations, however, the scattering rate deviates from linear scaling with oxygen content, suggesting limitations of independent-defect and pristine-phonon descriptions. These results demonstrate an efficient approach using pretrained MLIPs to quantify defect-limited thermal transport and provide insights into impurity effects beyond the dilute-defect approximation.

对声子和相变的机器学习原子间势进行有针对性的微调#

原题：Targeted fine-tuning of machine-learning interatomic potentials for phonons and phase transitions
作者：Jonas Grandel、Philipp Benner、Janine George。npj Computational Materials，2026-10-07

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

对声子和相变的机器学习原子间势进行有针对性的微调

English Abstract

Machine-learning interatomic potentials are widely used as computationally efficient surrogates for density functional theory in atomistic simulations, enabling large-scale and long-time modeling of materials systems. Here, we investigate how different fine-tuning strategies affect the prediction of harmonic phonon band structures, thermal and elastic properties, and the potential-energy surface along unstable phonon modes. We show that substantial improvements can be achieved with very limited additional data, with as few as 10 material-specific training structures. We investigate targetedL2-SP (L2-TSP) for this application, combining regularization toward the pre-trained model with selective fine-tuning of the first two representation-building layers, alongside transfer learning and multihead fine-tuning, with an additional comparison to low-rank LoRA for phonon prediction. Across 53 materials,L2-TSP achieves the most consistent overall performance, reducing phonon errors while also improving predictions of thermodynamic and elastic properties. Importantly, models with similar harmonic phonon accuracy can differ substantially in their prediction of dynamical instabilities and phase-transition behavior. Among the investigated approaches,L2-TSP shows the best overall agreement in dynamical-stability classification and in recovering the associated DFT reference phases, demonstrating improved generalization beyond the fine-tuning region. We implementL2-SP andL2-TSP inEquitrain, a model-agnostic software package for training and fine-tuning machine-learning interatomic potentials that can be applied beyond the MACE architecture considered here.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

准确预测与温度相关的弹性对于评估高温条件下的难熔碳化物非常重要，但从头算分子动力学 (AIMD) 的计算成本限制了跨成分和温度的系统研究。通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIP）提供了一种有效的替代方案，但其对该任务的准确性尚未充分确定。在这里，我们将九个 uMLIP 与 300 至 1200 K 之间的五种二元碳化物和两种高熵 (HE) 碳化物的一致 AIMD 参考数据进行基准测试。弹性常数和相应的体积模量、剪切模量和杨氏模量是使用应力应变分子动力学获得的，明确采样了热原子运动和超出热膨胀范围的非谐波效应。我们分别评估绝对弹性和标准化热软化。 MACE-MH-1 实现了最低的总体平均绝对百分比误差 (5.7%)。 MACE-MH-1 和 DPA4-Mini 能够最准确地再现热软化，平均偏差分别为 2.5 个百分点和 2.3 个百分点。所有模型通常都会低估刚度，相对于 AIMD 参考而言较大的平衡体积可能会导致大多数模型出现这种趋势。 C12 表现出最大的模型相关误差。 HE 碳化物的描述精度与二元碳化物相当，表明化学复杂性没有明显的精度损失。相反，精度随过渡金属成分的不同而变化，其中 V 族碳化物，特别是 TaC，带来了最大的挑战。这些结果确定了碳化物有限温度弹性的有前景的预训练模型，并说明了为什么必须独立评估准确的绝对刚度和热软化。

English Abstract

Accurate prediction of temperature-dependent elasticity is important for assessing refractory carbides under high-temperature conditions, but the computational cost of ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) limits systematic investigations across compositions and temperatures. Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) offer an efficient alternative, yet their accuracy for this task remains insufficiently established. Here, we benchmark nine uMLIPs against consistent AIMD reference data for five binary and two high-entropy (HE) carbides between 300 and 1200 K. Elastic constants and the corresponding bulk, shear, and Young's moduli are obtained using stress-strain molecular dynamics, explicitly sampling thermal atomic motion and anharmonic effects beyond thermal expansion alone. We assess absolute elastic properties and normalized thermal softening separately. MACE-MH-1 achieves the lowest overall mean absolute percentage error (5.7%). MACE-MH-1 and DPA4-Mini reproduce thermal softening most accurately, with mean deviations of 2.5 and 2.3 percentage points, respectively. All models generally underestimate stiffness, with larger equilibrium volumes relative to the AIMD reference likely contributing to this trend for most models. C12 exhibits the largest model-dependent errors. The HE carbides are described with accuracy comparable to that of the binary carbides, indicating no apparent accuracy penalty from chemical complexity. Accuracy instead varies with transition-metal composition, with group-V carbides, particularly TaC, presenting the greatest challenge. These results identify promising pretrained models for finite-temperature elasticity in carbides and show why accurate absolute stiffness and thermal softening must be assessed independently.

NEMORA：用于远程原子学习的神经等变多极算子#

原题：NEMORA: Neural Equivariant Multipole Operators for Long-Range Atomistic Learning
作者：Jay L. Kaplan、Samuel Varner、Rebecca Willett、Juan J. de Pablo。arXiv · physics，2026-10-09

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：equivariant graph neural network、graph neural networks、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

等变图神经网络已成为机器学习原子间势的基础架构，以一小部分计算成本接近量子化学精度。这些模型准确地描述了局部原子环境，但有限的空间截止截断了远程信息流，并且堆叠消息传递层可能导致过度平滑和过度挤压。现有的远程扩展要么规定固定的分析传播内核，要么将远程通信限制为标量或保度通道，要么只是近似等变，要么会产生超线性计算成本。将可学习的远程等变传输与多尺度多体表达性以及大型系统的有效扩展相结合仍然是一个核心挑战。我们引入神经等变多极算子（NEMORA），它是快速多极方法（FMM）的神经等变扩展，用于学习远程张量表示。 NEMORA 将 FMM 的分析多极展开和平移算子推广到自适应空间层次结构上学习的等变对应项。其算子耦合角度并在长度尺度上形成多体相互作用，保留 FMM 的分层组织和分析径向因素作为物理感应偏差，同时学习数据依赖的远程耦合。 NEMORA 以线性时间和内存复杂性进行评估，使其能够处理比其他长程方法更大的系统，达到数十万个原子，并且它增强了对称约束和无约束的短程主干。在非本地基准测试中，它相对于短程骨干网，分别减少了一个数量级以上和三个数量级的力和能量误差，这在精度上优于现有的远程扩展或具有竞争力。

English Abstract

Equivariant graph neural networks have emerged as foundational architectures for machine-learned interatomic potentials, approaching quantum-chemical accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost. These models describe local atomic environments accurately, but finite spatial cutoffs truncate long-range information flow, and stacking message-passing layers can lead to over-smoothing and over-squashing. Existing long-range extensions either prescribe a fixed analytical propagation kernel, restrict long-range communication to scalars or degree-preserving channels, are only approximately equivariant, or incur super-linear computational cost. Combining learnable long-range equivariant transport with multiscale many-body expressivity and efficient scaling for larger systems remains a central challenge. We introduce Neural Equivariant Multipole Operators (NEMORA), a neural equivariant extension of the Fast Multipole Method (FMM) for learning long-range tensorial representations. NEMORA generalizes the FMM's analytical multipole expansion and translation operators to learned equivariant counterparts on an adaptive spatial hierarchy. Its operators couple angular degrees and form many-body interactions across length scales, retaining the FMM's hierarchical organization and analytical radial factors as physical inductive biases while learning data-dependent long-range couplings. NEMORA evaluates in linear time and memory complexity, allowing it to treat larger systems than other long-range methods reaching hundreds of thousands of atoms, and it augments both symmetry-constrained and unconstrained short-range backbones. On non-local benchmarks, it reduces force and energy errors relative to the short-range backbones by over an order of magnitude and up to three orders of magnitude, respectively, which is better than or competitive with existing long-range extensions in accuracy.

通过神经网络势的分子动力学研究 V-4Ti-4Cr 合金的机械性能#

原题：Mechanical properties of V-4Ti-4Cr alloy from molecular dynamics with a neural-network potential
作者：G. S. Demyanov、D. V. Minakov、S. B. Saltykov、P. R. Levashov、N. M. Chtchelkatchev。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-07

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势能够对与聚变应用相关的钒合金进行大规模原子模拟，但在多组分系统中可靠的训练和验证仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们使用两阶段工作流程为 V-Ti-Cr 系统开发基于描述符的 DeepMD-DPA1 潜力：由 MatterSim 基础模型驱动的广泛配置采样，然后微调到使用 VASP 计算的密度泛函理论数据。该模型的能量均方根误差为 9.2 meV/原子，力分量的均方根误差为 0.23 eV/埃。利用大单元 LAMMPS 模拟中的这一潜力，我们计算了 V-4Ti-xCr 和 V-xTi-4Cr 合金在 T = 300 K 和 T = 1073 K 时的杨氏模量、体积模量和泊松比。我们发现，增加 Cr 含量会增加合金的弹性模量，而增加 Ti 含量会降低弹性模量；所有成分在 1073 K 时都比在 300 K 时软。两相模拟得出纯 V 的熔化温度为 1950 K，与实验数据非常吻合。

English Abstract

Machine-learning interatomic potentials enable large-scale atomistic simulations of vanadium alloys relevant to fusion applications, but reliable training and validation remain challenging in multicomponent systems. Here, we develop a descriptor-based DeepMD-DPA1 potential for the V-Ti-Cr system using a two-stage workflow: broad configuration sampling driven by the MatterSim foundation model followed by fine-tuning to density-functional-theory data computed with VASP. The model achieves root-mean-square errors of 9.2 meV/atom for energies and 0.23 eV/angstrom for force components. Using this potential in large-cell LAMMPS simulations, we compute Young's modulus, bulk modulus, and Poisson's ratio for V-4Ti-xCr and V-xTi-4Cr alloys at T = 300 K and T = 1073 K. We find that increasing Cr fraction increases the elastic moduli of the alloy, while increasing Ti fraction decreases them; all compositions are softer at 1073 K than at 300 K. Two-phase simulations give a melting temperature for pure V of 1950 K, in good agreement with experimental data.

基于炼金术谐波近似的等电子双原子学潜力：Δ-机器学习的基础基线#

原题：Alchemical harmonic approximation based potential for iso-electronic diatomics: Foundational baseline for Δ-machine learning

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们引入了固定原子间距离 d₀ 的电荷中性等电子双原子的绝对电子能的炼金谐波近似（AHA）。为了解释距离的变化，我们将 AHA 与 Ansatz 结合起来以获得电子结合势，E(d)=(Eᵤ-Eₛ) left(fracE_c-EₛEᵤ-Eₛright)sqrtd/d₀+Eₛ，其中 Eᵤ、E_c、Eₛ 对应于联合原子的能量、d₀ 处的校准以及无限分离原子的总和，分别。我们的模型覆盖了由距离和核电荷差跨越的整个二维电子势能表面，从中仅绘制一个单点（具有核电荷 Z1、Z2 和距离 d0 的元素）来校准 E_c。使用 pbe0/cc-pVDZ 的参考数据，我们提供了所有具有 8、10、12、14 个电子的中性双原子的电子基态的数值证据。我们通过与最相关的结合范围（0.7 - 2.5 A）内的传统原子间势（谐波振荡器、Lennard-Jones 和 Morse）进行比较来评估模型的有效性，并发现如果仅限于单个双原子，则具有可比的准确性，并且当外推到整个等电子系列时，预测能力显着提高。我们还使用我们的模型作为基线研究了电子绝对能量的 Δ 学习。该基线模型带来了系统性改进，有效地将达到化学精度所需的训练数据需求减少了一个数量级，从 sim1000 到 sim100。相比之下，使用 AHA+Morse 作为基线几乎不会带来任何改进，有时甚至会降低预测能力。推断不可见 CO 的能量在直接学习中收敛到 sim0.1 Ha 的预测误差，在我们的基线中收敛到 sim3 Ha。

English Abstract

We introduce the alchemical harmonic approximation (AHA) of the absolute electronic energy for charge-neutral iso-electronic diatomics at fixed interatomic distance d₀. To account for variations in distance, we combine AHA with this Ansatz for the electronic binding potential, E(d)=(Eᵤ-Eₛ) left(fracE_c-EₛEᵤ-Eₛright)sqrtd/d₀+Eₛ, where Eᵤ,E_c,Eₛ correspond to the energies of united atom, calibration at d₀, and sum of infinitely separated atoms, respectively. Our model covers the entire two-dimensional electronic potential energy surface spanned by distance and difference in nuclear charge from which only one single point (with elements of nuclear charge Z₁,Z₂ and distance d₀) is drawn to calibrate E_c. Using reference data from pbe0/cc-pVDZ, we present numerical evidence for the electronic ground-state of all neutral diatomics with 8, 10, 12, 14 electrons. We assess the validity of our model by comparison to legacy interatomic potentials (Harmonic oscillator, Lennard-Jones, and Morse) within the most relevant range of binding (0.7 - 2.5 A), and find comparable accuracy if restricted to single diatomics, and significantly better predictive power when extrapolating to the entire iso-electronic series. We also investigated Δ-learning of the electronic absolute energy using our model as baseline. This baseline model results in a systematic improvement, effectively reducing training data needs for reaching chemical accuracy by up to an order of magnitude from sim1000 to sim100. By contrast, using AHA+Morse as a baseline hardly leads to any improvement, and sometimes even deteriorates the predictive power. Inferring the energy of unseen CO converges to a prediction error of sim0.1 Ha in direct learning, and sim3 Ha with our baseline.

克服原子机器学习的体序势的局限性#

原题：Overcoming the limitations of body-ordered potentials for atomistic machine learning
作者：Filippo Bigi、Michelangelo Domina、Maximilian Lloyd Ach、Sergey N. Pozdnyakov、Paolo Pegolo、Joseph W. Abbott 等 8 人。arXiv · physics，2026-10-09

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：机器学习原子间势已成为原子模拟中不可或缺的工具。最流行的模型严重依赖于物理先验，例如局部性、平滑性和对称性，这些先验被认为可以提高训练模型的准确性和可转移性。然而，最近的证据表明，在数据丰富的情况下，从数据中学习对称性的无约束模型可以实现非常有竞争力的准确性和计算效率，而无需牺牲稳定性和泛化能力。许多等变对称体系结构历史上依赖于这样的假设：原子间势可以通过收敛簇展开来很好地近似，即与原子对、三元组、四元组和高阶元组相关的项的分解。我们描…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势已成为原子模拟中不可或缺的工具。最流行的模型严重依赖于物理先验，例如局部性、平滑性和对称性，这些先验被认为可以提高训练模型的准确性和可转移性。然而，最近的证据表明，在数据丰富的情况下，从数据中学习对称性的无约束模型可以实现非常有竞争力的准确性和计算效率，而无需牺牲稳定性和泛化能力。许多等变对称体系结构历史上依赖于这样的假设：原子间势可以通过收敛簇展开来很好地近似，即与原子对、三元组、四元组和高阶元组相关的项的分解。我们描述了一种结合了局部性、平滑性和对称性先验的架构，但由于计算效率高的张量积公式，它并不假设体有序序列的低阶截断。

English Abstract

Machine-learning interatomic potentials have become indispensable tools in atomistic simulations. The most popular models rely heavily on physical priors, such as locality, smoothness and symmetry, that are assumed to improve the accuracy and transferability of the trained models. However, recent evidence suggests that, in the data-rich regime, unconstrained models that learn symmetry from the data can achieve very competitive accuracy and computational efficiency, without sacrificing stability and generalization power. Many equivariant symmetric architectures historically rely on the assumption that the interatomic potential can be approximated well by a convergentcluster expansion, i.e., a decomposition in terms associated to atomic pairs, triplets, quadruplets, and higher-order tuples. We describe an architecture that incorporates locality, smoothness, and symmetry priors, but that -- thanks to a computationally efficient tensor product formulation -- does not assume low-order truncation of the body-ordered series.

使用卷积神经网络预测复杂介电谱的等离子体极表示#

原题：Predicting plasmonic pole representations of complex dielectric spectra using convolutional neural networks
作者：Dario A. Leon。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-09
🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：从介电谱中提取能量位置、展宽和光谱权重等光谱特性对于解释多体物理学中的集体电子激发至关重要。传统方法通常依赖于非线性拟合过程，该过程可能需要大量计算，并且对初始化和拟合选择高度敏感。在这项工作中，我们开发了一个卷积神经网络框架，用于使用多极帕德表示将介电谱直接反演为其底层等离子体极结构。该网络不是在与一组特定材料相关的受限光谱数据库上进行训练，而是完全根据具有随机参数的分析多极-Padé 表达式生成的合成光谱进行训练。这使得网络能够以公…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

从介电谱中提取能量位置、展宽和光谱权重等光谱特性对于解释多体物理学中的集体电子激发至关重要。传统方法通常依赖于非线性拟合过程，该过程可能需要大量计算，并且对初始化和拟合选择高度敏感。在这项工作中，我们开发了一个卷积神经网络框架，用于使用多极帕德表示将介电谱直接反演为其底层等离子体极结构。该网络不是在与一组特定材料相关的受限光谱数据库上进行训练，而是完全根据具有随机参数的分析多极-Padé 表达式生成的合成光谱进行训练。这使得网络能够以公正的方式学习光谱及其特征之间的一般映射，而不需要从真实材料中派生的大型、特定于材料的数据集。我们证明，经过综合训练的网络可推广到复杂的第一原理和实验介电谱，在单次前向传递中高精度地提取基础极参数。这种方法为传统非线性拟合提供了一种高效、稳健的替代方案，能够对不同材料和应用的介电谱进行高通量、自动化分析。

English Abstract

Extracting spectral properties such as energy position, broadening, and spectral weight from dielectric spectra is critical for interpreting collective electronic excitations in many-body physics. Conventional approaches typically rely on nonlinear fitting procedures that can be computationally demanding and highly sensitive to initialization and fitting choices. In this work, we develop a convolutional neural-network framework for the direct inversion of dielectric spectra into their underlying plasmonic pole structures using a multipole-Padé representation. Rather than training on a constrained database of spectra associated with a specific set of materials, the network is trained entirely on synthetic spectra generated from the analytical multipole-Padé expression with randomized parameters. This enables the network to learn the general mapping between the spectra and their features in an unbiased way, without requiring large, material-specific datasets derived from real materials. We demonstrate that the synthetically trained network generalizes to complex first-principles and experimental dielectric spectra, extracting the underlying pole parameters with high accuracy in a single forward pass. This approach provides an efficient and robust alternative to conventional nonlinear fitting, enabling high-throughput, automated analysis of dielectric spectra across diverse materials and applications.

PINN-Phase：用于曲率驱动多相场演化的物理信息神经网络#

原题：PINN-Phase: A physics-informed neural network for curvature-driven multiphase-field evolution
作者：Seifallah Elfetni、Sascha Thinius、Peter H. Seeberger、Theodore Tyrikos-Ergas。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-10-06
🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：当许多相关情况必须长期演化时，多晶微结构的相场模拟变得昂贵。我们引入了 PINN-Phase，这是一种基于物理的神经时间积分器，它可以从初始条件推进整个多相场，并在每一步强制执行相界和单位和。在报告的显式多相场路径上，它从其自己的预测状态的控制残差中学习，而在损失或检查点选择中没有后初始条件参考状态。固定的 25 粒模型在八个预期固定的看不见的微结构中的七个以及两种故意非典型应力情况下满足完整的预定义标准，最终标签不一致率为 0.94-…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

当许多相关情况必须长期演化时，多晶微结构的相场模拟变得昂贵。我们引入了 PINN-Phase，这是一种基于物理的神经时间积分器，它可以从初始条件推进整个多相场，并在每一步强制执行相界和单位和。在报告的显式多相场路径上，它从其自己的预测状态的控制残差中学习，而在损失或检查点选择中没有后初始条件参考状态。固定的 25 粒模型在八个预期固定的看不见的微结构中的七个以及两种故意非典型应力情况下满足完整的预定义标准，最终标签不一致率为 0.94-3.71%。在原始 128³ 分辨率下，在 4 个初始微结构上训练的八粒模型在经过 24,000 个自主步骤（其代表训练范围的 5.86 倍）后，在训练案例中保留了 98.79% 的标签一致性和准确的幸存者身份。在六个预期固定的未见过的 128³ 初始条件上，该范围内最后保存状态的标签不一致为 0.72-2.11%；在自主进化到 24,000 步（即代表训练范围的 5.86 倍）后，六分之五保留了准确的最终幸存者集。在 64 粒时，最终不一致率从初步评估中的 6.09% 下降到继续训练后的 3.97%，并精确恢复 21 粒幸存者集。这些结果证明了结构上可接受的长视野神经积分和固定基准系列内的直接初始条件转移。

English Abstract

Phase-field simulation of polycrystalline microstructures becomes costly when many related cases must be evolved over long times. We introduce PINN-Phase, a physics-informed neural time integrator that advances the full multiphase field from its initial condition and enforces phase bounds and unit sum at every step. On the reported explicit multiphase-field path, it learns from the governing residual on its own predicted states, without post-initial-condition reference states in the loss or checkpoint selection. A fixed 25-grain model satisfies the complete predefined criteria in seven of eight prospectively fixed unseen microstructures and in both deliberately atypical stress cases, with 0.94-3.71% terminal label disagreement. At native 128³ resolution, an eight-grain model trained on four initial microstructures retains 98.79% label agreement and exact survivor identities on a training case after 24,000 autonomous steps, 5.86 times its represented training horizon. On six prospectively fixed unseen 128³ initial conditions, label disagreement at the last saved state within that horizon is 0.72-2.11%; after autonomous evolution to 24,000 steps, or 5.86 times the represented training horizon, five of six retain the exact terminal survivor set. At 64 grains, terminal disagreement decreases from 6.09% in the primary evaluation to 3.97% after continued training, with exact recovery of the 21-grain survivor set. These results demonstrate structurally admissible long-horizon neural integration and direct initial-condition transfer within fixed benchmark families.

CrystalJev：用原子基础模型快速和慢速思考材料发现#

原题：CrystalJev: thinking fast and slow with atomistic foundation models for materials discovery
作者：Peng Kang、Zhen Li、Yu Liu、Lei Zheng、Huibin Xu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-07

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

原子基础模型对数百万种假设材料进行分类，但用作缓慢的模拟器，其阈值能量按表面值计算。他们最好被解读为快速决策者。 CrystalJev 对每个非松弛结构查询一次冻结的原子间势，并通过校准概率、有限样本保证以及何时缓慢思考的规则来回答键入的问题。在 65 个 Matbench Discovery 模型中，“稳定”判定是一种变相的概率，由模型的错误和候选群体来解释。一旦经过训练，一次前向传递的决策几乎与松弛的成本只有其三十分之一，而信息价值理论仅在决策可以改变的地方发送较慢的计算。同一层回答了电子、机械和分子问题。在一项包含 700 个新密度泛函计算的注册前瞻性测试中，仅根据现有数据校准的单遍预测最多高估了未见过的候选者的稳定比例 (5.8%) 2.1 个百分点。

English Abstract

Atomistic foundation models triage millions of hypothetical materials but are used as slow simulators, their thresholded energies taken at face value. They are better read as fast decision-makers. CrystalJev queries a frozen interatomic potential once per unrelaxed structure and answers typed questions with calibrated probabilities, finite-sample guarantees and a rule for when to think slowly. Across 65 Matbench Discovery models, a 'stable' call is a probability in disguise, explained by a model's errors and the candidate population. Once trained, one forward pass decides nearly as well as a relaxation at a thirtieth of its cost, and a value-of-information theory sends slower computation only where decisions can change. The same layer answers electronic, mechanical and molecular questions. In a registered prospective test with 700 new density-functional calculations, single-pass forecasts calibrated only on existing data over-stated the stable fraction of unseen candidates (5.8%) by at most 2.1 percentage points.

Zatom-2：用于跨领域生成建模的原子数据多任务预训练#

原题：Zatom-2: Multitask Pretraining on Atomistic Data for Generative Modeling across Domains

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

统一原子建模有潜力通过桥接数据丰富的化学领域和数据稀缺的生物领域来加速化学、材料科学和生物学的发现。然而，现有的原子建模生成方法仍然高度专业化于科学学科（化学与生物学），或者没有利用大量有机（分子）和无机（材料）数据进行通用预训练。为此，我们引入了 Zatom-2，这是一种原子生成模型，对来自 OMol25 和 OMat24 电子结构数据集的约 500 万个结构进行了预训练。 Zatom-2 采用多尺度 Transformer 架构以及条件流匹配，支持力调节和基础预训练任务，例如生成、结构预测以及分子和材料能量和力的预测。根据经验，Zatom-2 比 Zatom-1 实现了更好的分子分布保真度，并在现有分子和材料生成基准上实现了强大的性能。 Zatom-2 展示了在低力和高力状态下控制样本生成的能力，并通过联合生成预测预训练和迁移学习增强低数据环境中的蛋白质生成，将长度外推设置中的蛋白质骨架可设计性从没有预训练的 67.8% 提高到对 2,000 个蛋白质结构域进行微调后的 74.8%。

English Abstract

Unified atomistic modeling has the potential to accelerate discovery in chemistry, materials science, and biology by bridging data-rich chemical domains and data-scarce biological contexts. However, existing generative approaches to atomistic modeling remain highly specialized to scientific disciplines (chemistry vs. biology) or do not leverage both high-volume organic (molecule) and inorganic (material) data for general-purpose pretraining. To this end, we introduce Zatom-2, an atomistic generative model pretrained on approximately five million structures from the OMol25 and OMat24 electronic structure datasets. Zatom-2 features a multiscale Transformer architecture coupled with conditional flow matching that supports force conditioning and foundational pretraining tasks such as generation, structure prediction, and prediction of molecular and material energies and forces. Empirically, Zatom-2 achieves better molecular distribution fidelity than Zatom-1 and achieves strong performance on existing molecule and material generation benchmarks. Zatom-2 demonstrates the ability to control sample generation across low- and high-force regimes, and enhances protein generation in a low-data setting through joint generative-predictive pretraining and transfer learning, increasing protein backbone designability in a length extrapolation setting from 67.8% without pretraining to 74.8% after finetuning on 2,000 protein domains.

等变量生成扩散学习和推广无定形氧化物的结构集合#

原题：Equivariant generative diffusion learns and generalizes the structural ensemble of amorphous oxides
作者：Jun Jiang、Ian Berry、James N. Fry、Hai-Ping Cheng。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-06

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：非晶材料是统计系综，而不是确定的结构，传统的密度泛函 (DFT) 和机器学习电势模拟仅对该系综的一小部分进行采样。我们提出了一个 SE(3) 等变去噪扩散模型，该模型可以学习非晶氧化物的构型分布，因此该模型本身就是结构数据库。学习是数据高效的。在 1,781 个 DFT 配置上训练的模型足以重现部分径向分布函数、配位统计和键角分布，并以与最便宜的经典对势相当的成本生成超过 3×10⁵ 原子的模型。训练后的模型可以提出用于第一原理松弛的非…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

非晶材料是统计系综，而不是确定的结构，传统的密度泛函 (DFT) 和机器学习电势模拟仅对该系综的一小部分进行采样。我们提出了一个 SE(3) 等变去噪扩散模型，该模型可以学习非晶氧化物的构型分布，因此该模型本身就是结构数据库。学习是数据高效的。在 1,781 个 DFT 配置上训练的模型足以重现部分径向分布函数、配位统计和键角分布，并以与最便宜的经典对势相当的成本生成超过 3×10⁵ 原子的模型。训练后的模型可以提出用于第一原理松弛的非晶原子结构，以探索构型空间。例如，它将非晶 Zr-Ta-O 结构定位为比先前已知的最小值低 36 meV/原子。生成还可以超出训练条件，扩展到非化学计量成分、其他质量密度、界面和掺杂。第一性原理验证证实了生成可以被引导至所需的能量，并表明去噪训练损失不会对生成质量进行排名，因为两者衡量的是不同的东西。

English Abstract

Amorphous materials are statistical ensembles rather than definitive structures, and conventional density-functional (DFT) and machine-learned-potential simulations sample only a small part of that ensemble. We present an SE(3)-equivariant denoising-diffusion model that learns the configurational distribution of amorphous oxides, so the model itself is the structure database. The learning is data efficient. A model trained on 1,781 DFT configurations suffices to reproduce partial radial distribution functions, coordination statistics and bond-angle distributions, and to generate models of over 3×10⁵ atoms at a cost comparable to that of the cheapest classical pair potentials. The trained model can propose amorphous atomic structures for first-principles relaxation to explore the configuration space. For example, it locates an amorphous Zr-Ta-O structure 36 meV/atom below the previously known minimum. Generation can also extend beyond trained conditions to non-stoichiometric compositions, other mass densities, interfaces, and doping. First-principles verification confirms that generation can be steered to a requested energy, and shows that the denoising training loss does not rank generative quality, because the two measure different things.

深度学习驱动框架，用于在竞争强度要求下优化多晶微观结构#

原题：Deep learning driven framework for optimization of polycrystalline microstructures under competing strength requirements
作者：Ashwini Gupta、Indrashish Saha、Lori Graham-Brady、Tamer A. Zaki。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-08
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配0

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：为了增强机械性能而进行的多晶微结构的计算设计需要对随机形态进行重复的高保真模拟，这使得传统的晶体塑性有限元 (CPFE) 方法的优化成本过高。在这里，我们开发了一个深度学习驱动的框架，用于在竞争的准静态和动态性能要求下优化多晶微观结构。 3D U-Net 代理将多晶铜微观结构直接映射到板块撞击模拟的全场速度历史，保留评估动态性能和询问潜在波相互作用和失效机制所需的空间和时间分辨率。该替代物与随机微观结构生成和无导数优化相结合，以有效地导…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

为了增强机械性能而进行的多晶微结构的计算设计需要对随机形态进行重复的高保真模拟，这使得传统的晶体塑性有限元 (CPFE) 方法的优化成本过高。在这里，我们开发了一个深度学习驱动的框架，用于在竞争的准静态和动态性能要求下优化多晶微观结构。 3D U-Net 代理将多晶铜微观结构直接映射到板块撞击模拟的全场速度历史，保留评估动态性能和询问潜在波相互作用和失效机制所需的空间和时间分辨率。该替代物与随机微观结构生成和无导数优化相结合，以有效地导航设计空间。为了在优化过程中监控替代可靠性，我们引入了基于最大均值差异（MMD）的分布一致可靠性区域（DCRR），以量化训练数据的分布变化。该框架针对多个设计问题进行了演示，包括剥落和屈服强度的约束优化以及逆向设计。使用 CPFE 模拟对最优设计进行验证，所有优化案例的预测误差均小于 3%，而与直接基于 CPFE 的优化相比，代理驱动优化将累积评估时间减少了大约四个数量级。尽管此处演示的是针对散裂和屈服强度的多晶微结构设计，但该框架广泛适用于优化问题，其中高保真度全场模拟成本高昂，但空间和时间解析的响应信息对于评估、解释和优化候选设计至关重要。

English Abstract

Computational design of polycrystalline microstructures for enhanced mechanical performance requires repeated high-fidelity simulations over stochastic morphologies, rendering conventional crystal plasticity finite element (CPFE) approaches prohibitively expensive for optimization. Here, we develop a deep learning-driven framework for optimizing polycrystalline microstructures under competing quasi-static and dynamic performance requirements. A 3D U-Net surrogate maps polycrystalline copper microstructures directly to full-field velocity histories from plate-impact simulations, preserving the spatial and temporal resolution needed to evaluate dynamic performance and interrogate underlying wave interactions and failure mechanisms. The surrogate is coupled with stochastic microstructure generation and derivative-free optimization to efficiently navigate the design space. To monitor surrogate reliability during optimization, we introduce a distribution-consistent reliability region (DCRR) based on maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) to quantify distributional shift from the training data. The framework is demonstrated for multiple design problems, including constrained optimization of spall and yield strengths and inverse design. The optimal designs are validated using CPFE simulations with less than 3% prediction error across all optimization cases, while the surrogate-driven optimization reduces cumulative evaluation time by approximately four orders of magnitude compared with direct CPFE-based optimization. Although demonstrated here for the design of polycrystalline microstructures targeting spall and yield strengths, the framework is broadly applicable to optimization problems where high-fidelity full-field simulations are prohibitively expensive but spatially and temporally resolved response information is essential for evaluating, interpreting, and optimizing candidate designs.

晶体发生器离散组合通道的强化学习：验证增益和奖励黑客#

原题：Reinforcement Learning on the Discrete Composition Channel of a Crystal Generator: Validated Gains and Reward Hacking
作者：Pawan Prakash、Philipp Höllmer、Addis Fuhr、Peter Hirschfeld、P. Ganesh、Stefano Martiniani 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-06
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配0

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：逆向材料设计是计算材料发现的长期目标。晶体材料的生成模型通常经过训练以匹配结构数据库的分布，而其训练目标中没有任何内容指出特定的设计目标，例如目标属性。我们使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）通过强化学习将基于随机插值和离散流匹配的生成模型与一般黑盒奖励函数对齐。原子类型是由离散流生成的，GRPO 泛化的策略梯度直接作用于原子类型转换的可能性，这将我们的工作与之前用于扩散和基于流的晶体材料生成模型的强化学习方法区分开来。我们引入了一种奖励函数…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

逆向材料设计是计算材料发现的长期目标。晶体材料的生成模型通常经过训练以匹配结构数据库的分布，而其训练目标中没有任何内容指出特定的设计目标，例如目标属性。我们使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）通过强化学习将基于随机插值和离散流匹配的生成模型与一般黑盒奖励函数对齐。原子类型是由离散流生成的，GRPO 泛化的策略梯度直接作用于原子类型转换的可能性，这将我们的工作与之前用于扩散和基于流的晶体材料生成模型的强化学习方法区分开来。我们引入了一种奖励函数，根据社区基准的评估，该函数将亚稳态、独特和新颖结构 (mSUN) 的产量从预训练模型的 13.4% 提高到强化模型的 45.5%。我们的奖励还提高了基于潜在去噪扩散模型的晶体材料强化学习框架的性能。与此同时，我们发现直接增强原子型转变可能性可以实现奖励利用，而必须通过明确的保护措施来防止这种情况。相同的分析还暴露了社区指标中的差距。不同填料中的单元件结构被视为亚稳态、独特和新颖的材料，并且使 mSUN 膨胀而不会产生任何新化合物。稳定性声明的好坏取决于其参考船体。我们报告每个结果除以其背后的参考阶段的数量，并认为基准测试也应该这样做。

English Abstract

Inverse materials design is a long-standing goal of computational materials discovery. Generative models for crystalline materials are typically trained to match the distribution of a structure database, while nothing in their training objective points them at specific design goals such as targeted properties. We use group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) to align a generative model based on stochastic interpolants and discrete flow matching with general black-box reward functions through reinforcement learning. Atom types are generated by a discrete flow and the policy gradient of our generalization of GRPO directly acts on the likelihoods of the atom-type transitions, which differentiates our work from previous reinforcement-learning approaches for diffusion and flow-based generative models of crystalline materials. We introduce a reward function that raises the yield of metastable, unique and novel structures (mSUN) from 13.4% for the pretrained model to 45.5% for the reinforced model, as evaluated by a community benchmark. Our reward also improves the performance of a reinforcement learning framework for crystalline materials based on latent denoising diffusion models. At the same time, we find that directly reinforcing atom-type transition likelihoods enables reward exploitation that has to be prevented with explicit guards. The same analysis also exposes a gap in the community metric. Single-element structures in distinct packings are counted as metastable, unique and novel materials and inflate mSUN without yielding any new compounds. A stability claim is only as good as its reference hull. We report every result split by the number of reference phases behind it and argue that benchmarks should do the same.

根据本体物理性质对聚合物中原子氧侵蚀率进行数据驱动预测#

原题：Data-driven prediction of atomic-oxygen erosion yield in polymers from bulk physical properties
作者：Youngwoo Choi、Doeun Koo、Seungbum Hong、Hyun Jung Kim。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-07

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

原子氧 (AO) 会侵蚀低地球轨道上的聚合物表面，但耐久性数据有限。我们使用八种选定的整体特性和五种元素组成比评估了在 37 种 MISSE-2 聚合物上训练的回归系综。其存档留一法 R²(log) 为 0.768；该估计以使用完整训练表进行特征选择为条件。对于 MISSE-2 后的九种聚合物，整体得出 R²(log) = -0.037，所有预测均在两倍以内。训练算术平均基线也满足所有九个的容忍度，突显了其狭窄的目标范围提供的有限歧视。地面比较包括四个 PLAVIS 等级。对于未公开成分的训练外神秘聚合物，预测的侵蚀量在地面测量值的 1.92 倍以内。相互冲突的 PLAVIS 测量记录无法得出明确的残留侵蚀排序或地面验证分数。热残留物、SEM 对比度和表面化学提供了明显的观察证据；他们没有建立保护层机制。结果支持通过协调的测量记录和前瞻性固定输入进一步评估轨道数据筛选模型。

English Abstract

Atomic oxygen (AO) erodes polymer surfaces in low Earth orbit, but durability data are limited. We evaluated a regression ensemble trained on 37 MISSE-2 polymers using eight selected bulk properties and five elemental-composition ratios. Its archived leave-one-out R²(log) was 0.768; this estimate is conditional on feature selection using the complete training table. For nine post-MISSE-2 polymers, the ensemble gave R²(log) = -0.037 and all predictions were within a factor of two. The training arithmetic-mean baseline also met that tolerance for all nine, highlighting the limited discrimination provided by their narrow target range. Ground-based comparisons included four PLAVIS grades. For an out-of-training mystery polymer with undisclosed composition, the predicted erosion yield was within a factor of 1.92 of the ground-based measurement. Conflicting PLAVIS measurement records prevent a definitive residue-erosion ordering or ground-validation score. Thermal residue, SEM contrast and surface chemistry provide distinct observational evidence; they do not establish a protective-layer mechanism. The results support further evaluation of orbital-data screening models with reconciled measurement records and prospectively fixed inputs..

法学硕士辅助生成透明、开源的电化学器件多物理场模型#

原题：LLM-Assisted Generation of Transparent, Open-Source Multiphysics Models of Electrochemical Devices
作者：Sebastian Castro、Maya F. Schuchert、Spencer A. McCluskey、Eric W. Lees、Justin C. Bui。arXiv · physics，2026-10-08

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

多物理场连续介质模型是研究电化学装置的强大工具，可在计算机中设计反应器并解决控制装置性能但难以通过实验测量的局部 pH、电势和浓度场。然而，构建此类模型需要大量的数值专业知识或依赖专有软件。在这里，我们展示了前沿大型语言模型代理可以消除这种实现负担，同时将底层物理保持在研究人员的控制之下。使用多孔气体扩散电极中一维电化学 CO2 还原为 CO 作为测试用例，我们开发了一种机器可读的、人类指定的建模工具，其中包含控制方程、参数、数值方法、逻辑构建阶段和人类可验证的检查点。根据此规范，代理可在开源 Julia 中重复构建完整的多物理场模型。独立构建的模型（包括完全自主的代理构建的模型）与等效 COMSOL 实施一致，其误差在峰值 CO 部分电流密度的 0.7% 以内，并且彼此之间的误差在 0.04% 以内。系统地植入的错误证明了明确的可重复性规范的重要性，并揭示了代理在调试模型物理方面的能力和局限性。该框架建立了一种更加透明的多物理场建模方法，其中物理描述和控制方程（而不是专门的代码）成为计算模型开发的主要输入。

English Abstract

Multiphysics continuum models are powerful tools for studying electrochemical devices, enabling in silico reactor design and resolution of local pH, potential, and concentration fields that govern device performance but are difficult to measure experimentally. However, constructing such models requires substantial numerical expertise or reliance on proprietary software. Here, we show that frontier large language model agents can remove this implementation burden while keeping the underlying physics under researcher control. Using one-dimensional electrochemical CO2 reduction to CO in a porous gas diffusion electrode as a test case, we develop a machine-readable, human-specified modeling harness containing governing equations, parameters, numerical methods, logical build stages, and human-verifiable checkpoints. From this specification, the agent reproducibly constructs complete multiphysics models in open-source Julia. Independently built models, including fully autonomous agent-built models, agree with an equivalent COMSOL implementation to within 0.7% of the peak CO partial current density, and with one another to within 0.04%. Systematically planted errors demonstrate the importance of explicit specifications for reproducibility and reveal the agent's capabilities and limitations in debugging model physics. This framework establishes a more transparent approach to multiphysics modeling in which physical descriptions and governing equations, rather than specialized code, become the primary inputs for computational model development.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

同位素效应控制分馏并调节反应性，其应用范围从氢能到环境科学和催化，但预测它们需要解决与同位素相关的小自由能差异，这对于复杂系统中的传统路径积分模拟仍然非常具有挑战性。在这里，我们介绍 iso-EPIGS，这是一种基于质量可微神经网络构建的路径积分粗粒度框架，可重建质量和温度相关的路径积分质心自由能表面。学习表面上的经典分子动力学产生严格的同位素解析热力学，无需明确的路径积分采样。跨越气体、液体和晶相（包括液态水和草酸晶体）的基准，以接近经典的计算成本重现参考路径积分同位素自由能差异、焓和晶格参数。至关重要的是，仅针对全 H 和全 D 同位素体进行训练的 iso-EPIGS 对于未见过的部分氘化同位素体保持了高精度，证明了跨核质量的强大可转移性。 Iso-EPIGS 使复杂系统的精确同位素效应模拟变得可行，并为跨化学空间的同位素效应基础模型奠定了基础。

English Abstract

Isotope effects govern fractionation and modulate reactivity, with applications from hydrogen energy to environmental science and catalysis, yet predicting them requires resolving small isotope-dependent free-energy differences that remain very challenging for conventional path-integral simulations in complex systems. Here we introduce iso-EPIGS, a path-integral coarse-graining framework built on a mass-differentiable neural network that reconstructs the mass- and temperature-dependent path integral centroid free-energy surface. Classical molecular dynamics on the learned surface yields rigorous isotope-resolved thermodynamics without explicit path-integral sampling. Benchmarks spanning gas, liquid, and crystalline phases, including liquid water and oxalic acid crystal, reproduce reference path-integral isotope free-energy differences, enthalpies, and lattice parameters at near classical computational cost. Crucially, iso-EPIGS trained solely on all-H and all-D isotopologues retains high accuracy for unseen partially deuterated isotopologues, demonstrating robust transferability across nuclear masses. Iso-EPIGS makes accurate isotope-effect simulations feasible for complex systems and lays the groundwork for foundation models of isotope effects across chemical space.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

目前为钠离子电池（SIB）设计的阴极活性材料（CAM）存在工作电压低、能量密度有限、不可逆相变和固有电导率低等问题。在这项工作中，我们开发了可泛化和可转移的基于描述符的机器学习（ML）模型，以及基于组合和图的神经网络（NN）和转移学习（TL）模型，所有模型都在仅充电结构而不是充电/放电对的数据集上进行训练。使用由前四个经过训练的 ML 模型组成的委员会，将平均电压预测为目标属性。更重要的是，我们表明，不确定性量化可以通过包含当前委员会模型最不自信的结构来提高训练后续更高一代模型的候选选择的可靠性。通过这种方式，新的昂贵的从头开始数据可以提供原始模型最需要改进的区域的信息。我们展示了最终一代模型的可转移性，将其应用于由 Materials Project、AFLOW、OQMD 和 GNoME 策划的大型无钠无机主体结构数据库，用于大规模 SIB CAM 发现。还开发了一个综合性交互式在线数据库 NaCAM-Atlas，其中包含 14,843 个 SIB 电压大于 3 V 的 CAM。最后，通过电压分布、钠化/去钠化的结构鲁棒性和电子特性的明确、高通量第一原理计算，对排名靠前的候选 CAM 的子集进行了验证。这种集成的数据管理、ML 培训和改进以及第一原理验证策略共同建立了一个可扩展且可转移的框架，以加速为 SIB 及其他领域开发稳定、高压的 CAM。

English Abstract

Current cathode-active materials (CAMs) designed for sodium-ion batteries (SIBs) suffer from low operating voltages, limited energy density, irreversible phase transitions, and low intrinsic electrical conductivity. In this work, we have developed generalizable and transferable descriptor-based machine learning (ML) models, along with composition- and graph-based neural networks (NNs) and transfer learning (TL) models, all trained on a dataset of charged-only structures rather than charged/discharged pairs. Using a committee of the top four trained ML models, the mean voltage is predicted as the target property. More importantly, we show that uncertainty quantification can improve the reliability of candidate selection for training subsequent higher-generation models by including structures for which the current committee models are least confident. In this way, the new expensive ab initio data provide information in regions where the original models most need improvement. We have showcased the transferability of the final generation models by applying them to a large database of Na-free inorganic host structures curated from Materials Project, AFLOW, OQMD, and GNoME for at-scale SIB CAM discovery. A comprehensive interactive online database, NaCAM-Atlas, was also developed, containing 14,843 CAMs with voltages greater than 3 V for SIBs. Finally, a subset of the top-ranked candidate CAMs was validated through explicit, high-throughput first-principles calculations of voltage profiles, structural robustness upon sodiation/desodiation, and electronic properties. Together, this integrated data curation, ML training and improvement, and first-principles validation strategy establishes a scalable and transferable framework for accelerating the discovery of stable, high-voltage CAMs for SIBs and beyond.

分子特征的光谱分析：当更丰富的特征不能保证更好的泛化时#

原题：Spectral Analysis of Molecular Features: When Richer Features Do Not Guarantee Better Generalization

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：特征嵌入的光谱特性为模型泛化和表示质量提供了重要的见解。虽然深度学习模型广泛用于分子特性预测，但核方法在低数据范围内仍然具有竞争力，但它们的光谱行为在很大程度上尚未被探索。我们首次对不同表示形式的核脊回归进行全面光谱分析，包括分子指纹 (ECFP)、预训练 Transformer、图神经网络和 3D 描述符，并在 QM9、MoleculeNet 和 Therapeutics Data Commons 基准上进行评估。令人惊讶的是，更丰富…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

特征嵌入的光谱特性为模型泛化和表示质量提供了重要的见解。虽然深度学习模型广泛用于分子特性预测，但核方法在低数据范围内仍然具有竞争力，但它们的光谱行为在很大程度上尚未被探索。我们首次对不同表示形式的核脊回归进行全面光谱分析，包括分子指纹 (ECFP)、预训练 Transformer、图神经网络和 3D 描述符，并在 QM9、MoleculeNet 和 Therapeutics Data Commons 基准上进行评估。令人惊讶的是，更丰富的光谱特征并不总是能产生更好的泛化性能，这与自监督学习（SSL）中使用的常见表示启发法相矛盾。在四个光谱指标中，只有基于 ECFP 的内核与性能表现出严格的正相关性，特别是 MoleculeNet 上的 SSE、ID 和 SR。全局 3D 表示表现出混合行为；基于变压器的表示在所有四个指标中始终呈正值，而局部 3D 表示则显示出负趋势且没有显着相关性。截断分析进一步强调了这种差异：对于热力学目标的局部 3D 表示，需要不到 2% 的特征值，有时只需 0.02%，即可恢复 95% 的性能，而 ECFP 和变压器内核则需要更多的特征值。通过证明对任务和表示的强烈依赖，我们的结果挑战了更丰富的光谱本质上提高泛化的启发式，为评估 SSL 和标签有限的科学任务中的表示提供了新的指导。

English Abstract

The spectral properties of feature embeddings offer critical insights into model generalization and representation quality. While deep learning models are widely used for molecular property prediction, kernel methods remain competitive in low-data regimes, yet their spectral behavior is largely unexplored. We present the first comprehensive spectral analysis of kernel ridge regression across diverse representations, including molecular fingerprints (ECFP), pretrained transformers, graph neural networks, and 3D descriptors, evaluated on QM9, MoleculeNet, and Therapeutics Data Commons benchmarks. Surprisingly, richer spectral features do not consistently yield better generalization performance, contradicting common representation heuristics used in self-supervised learning (SSL). Across four spectral metrics, only ECFP-based kernels show a strictly positive correlation with performance, particularly SSE, ID, and SR on MoleculeNet. Global 3D representations exhibit mixed behavior; transformer-based representations are consistently positive across all four metrics, whereas local 3D representations show a negative trend and no significant correlations. Truncation analysis further emphasizes this disparity: for local 3D representations on thermodynamic targets, fewer than 2% of eigenvalues, and occasionally as few as 0.02%, are needed to recover 95% of performance, whereas ECFP and transformer kernels require significantly more. By demonstrating a strong dependence on both task and representation, our results challenge the heuristic that richer spectra inherently improve generalization, providing new guidance for evaluating representations in SSL and in label-limited scientific tasks.

用于小型化电容器的温度稳定高κ氧化物#

原题：Temperature-stable high- κ oxides for miniaturized capacitors
作者：Junlei Qi、Yiying Chen、Jincheng Qin、Bin Wei、Hao Pan、Tengfei Hu 等 22 人。Nature Electronics，2026-10-06
🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：机器学习辅助材料设计方法可以导航大型成分搜索空间和引导结构设计，从而可以识别用于小型化电容器的温度稳定、低损耗、高κ陶瓷。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习辅助材料设计方法可以导航大型成分搜索空间和引导结构设计，从而可以识别用于小型化电容器的温度稳定、低损耗、高κ陶瓷。

English Abstract

The miniaturization of capacitive components in advanced electronics requires dielectric materials that simultaneously have a high permittivity, low loss tangent and near-zero temperature coefficient of capacitance. However, improving one property typically negatively affects the others, and the design of new dielectrics is challenging due to the vast compositional space that could be searched. Here we report a hierarchical machine learning-guided strategy that first screens oxide candidates to identify promising structural families and then optimizes local atomic features to enhance the dielectric properties. The approach identifies tungsten bronze structures, followed by targeted bismuth and germanium co-substitution. The resulting Ba4(Nd0.5Bi0.5)9.33(Ti0.99Ge0.01)18O54ceramic had a dielectric constant of around 185, a loss tangent below 0.0005 and a temperature coefficient of ≤±30 ppm per degree Celsius. We also study the mechanism of the temperature-insensitive behaviour using in situ atomic-resolution scanning transmission electron microscopy and density functional theory analysis, and we find that strong Ge–O covalent bonds sustain off-centre polar displacements during thermal expansion. The material is used to create prototype single-layer chip capacitors with volumetric efficiency higher than leading commercial benchmarks.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

人工智能 (AI) 正在成为整个实验科学中越来越有用的工具，包括角度分辨光电子能谱 (ARPES)，它通常会生成大型、多维的电子结构数据集。人工智能和机器学习 (ML) 的最新进展为整个 ARPES 工作流程带来了新的机遇，从自动化样品制备和实时数据采集到实验后数据分析以及与理论计算的比较。尽管取得了这些进展，但仍然缺乏对 ARPES 工作流程不同阶段的 ML 应用程序、其功能和可靠性的全面审查。在这篇综述中，我们首先介绍与凝聚态物理和材料科学领域的实验学家最相关的机器学习方法。然后，我们遵循 ARPES 工作流程，在每个步骤中审查现有的 ML 应用程序，并讨论它们的优势、局限性和未来发展的潜力。我们还研究了当前的 ARPES 数据格局，其中有几个可用的开放数据库，但与 ImageNet 等大型共享数据集相比仍然相对较小且分散。鉴于这些限制，我们建议社区重点共享可进一步训练、适应特定任务和重新分发的预训练模型，同时致力于建立更大、标准化的开放 ARPES 数据集存储库。最后，我们使用六级实验室自动化框架讨论了 ARPES 工作流程中人工智能的未来前景，强调了迈向完全自主、自动驾驶 ARPES 实验室的机遇和挑战。

English Abstract

Artificial intelligence (AI) is becoming an increasingly useful tool across the experimental sciences, including angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), which routinely produces large, multidimensional datasets of electronic structure. Recent advances in AI and machine learning (ML) have opened new opportunities across the entire ARPES workflow, from automated sample preparation and real-time data acquisition to post-experiment data analysis and comparison with theoretical calculations. Despite this progress, a comprehensive review of ML applications, their capabilities, and reliability across the different stages of ARPES workflow is still lacking. In this review, we first introduce ML methods that are most relevant to experimentalists working in condensed matter physics and materials science. We then follow the ARPES workflow, reviewing existing ML applications at each step and discussing their advantages, limitations and potential for future development. We also examine the current ARPES data landscape, where several open databases are available but remain relatively small and fragmented compared with large, shared datasets such as ImageNet. Given these limitations, we suggest that the community focus on sharing pretrained models that can be further trained, adapted to specific tasks, and redistributed, while working toward a larger and standardized open ARPES dataset repository. Finally, we discuss our perspectives on the future of AI within the ARPES workflow using a six-level framework of laboratory automation, highlighting the opportunities and challenges in moving toward a fully autonomous, self-driving ARPES laboratory.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

为了补充在材料科学中提供概念、概念和实体的语义表示方面的许多努力，我们报告并说明了一个过程，通过这个过程，我们可以自动构建快速发展的机器学习领域的知识图谱，应用于材料属性的预测，重点是MLIP （机器学习原子间势能）。这种基于LLM的过程依赖于多个步骤，从文档和文章中的信息提取到使用SHACL约束来检测和纠正错误的验证循环。它是在逐个模型的基础上进行的，侧重于表示的一致性，因此可以进行迭代构建，从而促进新模型的添加。我们通过展示几个有趣的方面来说明这个过程，这些方面可以从Matbench Discovery排行榜中列出的模型构建的知识图表中查询。

English Abstract

Complementing the many efforts in providing semantic representations of concepts, notions, and entities in materials science, we report and illustrate a process by which we can automatically build a knowledge graph of the fast evolving field of machine learning applied to the prediction of material properties, focusing on MLIP (Machine Learning Interatomic Potential). This LLM-based process relies on multiple steps, from information extraction in documents and articles to a validation loop using SHACL constraints to detect and correct errors. It is carried out on a model-by-model basis, focusing on the consistency of representation, therefore enabling an iterative construction where the addition of new models is facilitated. We illustrate the process by showing a few interesting aspects that can be queried from a knowledge graph built from the models listed in the Matbench Discovery leaderboard.

用于原子模拟专家级自动化的可扩展Agentic框架#

原题：An Extensible Agentic Framework for Expert-Level Automation of Atomistic Simulations

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

传统上，原子模拟一直受到从头方法的计算缩放限制和经验力场的参数化开销的限制。最近出现的通用机器学习原子间电位显著减轻了这些瓶颈，以很小的计算成本在不同的化学空间提供近量子精度和可推广性。然而，这种转变将瓶颈转移到了人类层面：投入准备和数据分析等耗时的机械过程现在占据了研究生命周期的主导地位。我们介绍了Paimon ，这是一个用于材料优化和纳米级模拟的代理集成的平台。通过对专家级液体电解质模拟的数百次试验，我们表明Paimon通过抑制无声错误（合理但物理上不正确的结果）大大提高了代理工作流程的可靠性。Paimon在不修改现有药剂的情况下适应了一种新功能，我们通过整合外部开发的科学药剂来证明这一点。作为原子模拟的代理工具， Paimon在整个模拟生命周期中为研究人员提供了以科学为中心的连续工作流程。

English Abstract

Traditionally, atomistic simulation has been constrained by the computational scaling limits of ab initio methods and the parameterization overhead of empirical force fields. The recent emergence of universal machine learning interatomic potentials has significantly mitigated these bottlenecks, offering near-quantum accuracy and generalizability across diverse chemical spaces at a fraction of the computational cost. However, this shift has relocated the bottleneck to the human dimension: time-consuming mechanical processes, such as input preparation and data analysis, now dominate the research lifecycle. We introduce Paimon, a Platform for Agentic Integration in Materials Optimization and Nanoscale-simulations. Through hundreds of trials on an expert-level liquid electrolyte simulation, we show that Paimon substantially improves the reliability of agentic workflows by suppressing silent errors: plausible yet physically incorrect results. Paimon accommodates a new capability without modifying the agents already present, which we demonstrate by integrating an externally developed scientific agent. As an agent harness for atomistic simulations, Paimon affords researchers a continuous, science-centric workflow throughout the entire simulation lifecycle.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

即使机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 接近从头开始的精度，经典力场 (FF) 仍然是大规模模拟的主力。它们将总构型能量分解为简单有效的相互作用，其参数传统上是根据原子或键类型分配的，从而实现高效的模拟，但也限制了它们适应构型的能力。最近的机器学习方法通过将这些 FF 中的键合参数推断为局部原子环境的函数，提高了它们的准确性和可转移性，但仍然依赖于经验非键合参数进行实际模拟。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种统一的方法，textttgrappa-fullFF，它从头开始参考数据，同时强调一致地学习键合和非键合参数。通过静电势监督和促进电荷平衡的架构结合物理启发的正则化，我们的模型恢复了准确的电响应特性，在几何优化基准上实现了最先进的精度，并重现了经典和现有机器学习 FF 的构象采样，而不依赖于外部分配的非键合参数。

English Abstract

Classical force fields (FFs) remain the workhorse for large-scale simulations even as machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) approach ab initio accuracy. They decompose total configuration energies into simple effective interactions whose parameters are traditionally assigned based on atom or bond types, enabling efficient simulations but also limiting their ability to adapt across configurations. Recent machine learning approaches have improved the accuracy and transferability of bonded parameters in these FFs by inferring them as functions of local atomic environments, but still rely on empirical nonbonded parameters for practical simulations. In this work, we introduce a unified approach, textttgrappa-fullFF, which learns both bonded and nonbonded parameters emphconsistently and simultaneously from ab initio reference data. By incorporating physically inspired regularization via supervision of the electrostatic potential and an architecture that facilitates charge equilibration, our model recovers accurate electric response properties, achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on geometry optimization benchmarks, and reproduces the conformational sampling of both classical and existing machine-learned FFs, without relying on externally assigned nonbonded parameters.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

金属合金的机械性能由其微观结构和织构决定：晶粒的大小和形状以及晶体的取向。该结构又由配方、合金成分以及加工参数决定。合金开发向前推进这条链条，调整结构直到满足目标性能。逆向运行，从优化的结构到生产它的配方，仍然依赖于专业知识。我们问这种倒退的步骤是否可以学习。在 14 种合金的 107 种镁合金挤压条件的内部数据集上，每种条件都有光学显微照片和 X 射线纹理测量，我们比较了微观结构和纹理的三种描述符：传统的晶粒和纹理统计数据、来自预训练图像编码器的视觉嵌入以及晶粒网络上的图神经网络。每个预测头都与两个任务配对：给定工艺的合金成分（任务 A），以及给定成分的工艺参数（任务 B）。在 5 倍交叉验证下，传统描述符在 65% 的保留条件下识别出正确的合金，而始终猜测最常见合金的识别率只有 17%，而学习到的嵌入保持在 30% 以下。工艺参数是可恢复的，但噪音更大：与单独使用成分相比，微观结构将温度误差大致减半。因为只铸造了几种合金并且只使用了几种压力设置，所以两个答案都是离散的，并且从这些已知选项中挑选的头在尊重其顺序的同时比预测自由值的头工作得更好。

English Abstract

The mechanical properties of a metallic alloy are set by its microstructure and texture: the size and shape of its grains and the orientation of their crystals. That structure is in turn set by a recipe, the alloy composition together with the processing parameters. Alloy development runs this chain forwards, tuning the structure until a target property is met. Running it backwards, from an optimized structure to the recipe that would produce it, still relies on expert knowledge. We ask whether this backwards step can be learned. On an in-house dataset of 107 magnesium alloy extrusion conditions across 14 alloys, each with optical micrographs and an X-ray texture measurement, we compare three descriptors of microstructure and texture: conventional grain and texture statistics, a vision embedding from a pretrained image encoder, and a graph neural network on the grain network. Each is paired with prediction heads for two tasks: the alloy composition given the process (Task A), and the process parameters given the composition (Task B). Under 5-fold cross-validation, the conventional descriptors identify the correct alloy for 65% of held-out conditions, against 17% for always guessing the most common alloy, while the learned embeddings stay below 30%. The process parameters are recoverable but noisier: compared with using the composition alone, the microstructure roughly halves the temperature error. Because only a few alloys were cast and only a few press settings were used, both answers are discrete, and heads that pick from these known options, while respecting their order, worked better than heads that predict a free value.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

YRu3Si2 和 LaRu3Si2 具有 Kagome 金属中报道的最高电荷密度波 (CDW) 转变温度，TCDWapprox 800 和 400~K，传播矢量分别为 QCDWapprox(1/2,0,0) 和 (1/4,0,0)。这些记录值背后的微观机制仍未解决。在这里，我们将从机器学习力场驱动的分子动力学中提取的非谐波声子重正化与模式分辨电子声子耦合（EPC）分析结合起来，以确定这种机制。 CDW 由模式选择性 EPC 驱动：Ru 面外声子模式耦合 Ru-dₓz/dyz 和 Si-pₓ/p_y 轨道，声子线宽在 QCDW 处急剧峰值，而无特征的电子磁化率排除了费米表面嵌套。声子非谐性融化了 CDW，非谐声子谱再现了 QCDW 和 TCDW，与实验非常吻合。分子动力学模拟还可以可视化真实空间中 CDW 的熔化。化学键分析进一步表明，较小的 Y3⁺ 半径增强了 Ru-Ru 键，提高了晶格刚性，并导致 YRu₃Si2 的 TCDW 提高了两倍。我们的结果建立了 Kagome 金属 YRu₃Si2 和 LaRu₃Si2 中 CDW 形成和熔化的统一显微图像。

English Abstract

YRu₃Si₂ and LaRu₃Si₂ host the highest charge density wave (CDW) transition temperatures ever reported in kagome metals, TCDWapprox 800 and 400~K, with propagation vectors QCDWapprox(1/2,0,0) and (1/4,0,0), respectively. The microscopic mechanism behind these record values has remained unresolved. Here, we combine anharmonic phonon renormalization extracted from molecular dynamics driven by a machine-learned force field with mode-resolved electron-phonon coupling (EPC) analysis to identify this mechanism. The CDW is driven by mode-selective EPC: the Ru out-of-plane phonon modes couple the Ru-dₓz/dyz and Si-pₓ/p_y orbitals, and the phonon linewidth peaks sharply at QCDW, while the featureless electronic susceptibility rules out Fermi-surface nesting. Phonon anharmonicity melts the CDW, and the anharmonic phonon spectra reproduce both QCDW and TCDW in good agreement with experiments. Molecular dynamics simulations also visualize the CDW melting in real space. Chemical bonding analysis further shows that the smaller Y³⁺ radius strengthens the Ru-Ru bonds, enhancing lattice rigidity and accounting for the factor-of-two higher TCDW of YRu₃Si₂. Our results establish a unified microscopic picture of CDW formation and melting in kagome metals YRu₃Si₂ and LaRu₃Si₂.

原题：How Good Is DFT for Solid-Liquid Interfaces? A Comparison With the Random-Phase Approximation for Water on Graphene

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：固体界面处的水模拟通常在从接触角到摩擦等基本特性上存在分歧，具体取决于所使用的模型，无论是经验力场还是密度泛函理论 (DFT) 交换相关 (XC) 近似。然而，还没有准确的计算参考来确定哪一个（如果有的话）是可靠的。在这里，我们评估跨越雅各布天梯梯级的 XC 近似与石墨烯-水界面处的随机相位近似 (RPA)。使用在 DFT 和 RPA 数据上训练的机器学习势 (MLP)，我们实现了表征界面结构、润湿性、摩擦力和振动和频生成谱所需的广泛采…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

固体界面处的水模拟通常在从接触角到摩擦等基本特性上存在分歧，具体取决于所使用的模型，无论是经验力场还是密度泛函理论 (DFT) 交换相关 (XC) 近似。然而，还没有准确的计算参考来确定哪一个（如果有的话）是可靠的。在这里，我们评估跨越雅各布天梯梯级的 XC 近似与石墨烯-水界面处的随机相位近似 (RPA)。使用在 DFT 和 RPA 数据上训练的机器学习势 (MLP)，我们实现了表征界面结构、润湿性、摩擦力和振动和频生成谱所需的广泛采样。我们发现 XC 近似值与 RPA 以及彼此之间存在很大差异。为了理解这一点，我们引入了一个可转移、多可观察的评分框架，该框架将每个功能与 RPA 的协议提炼为单个度量。六个泛函成为研究石墨烯-水和其他色散主导界面的可靠起点，即 B3LYP-D3(0)、revPBE-D3(0)、revPBE-D3(BJ)、B97M-rV、r²SCAN 和 revPBE0-D3(0)。值得注意的是，这个排名并不遵循基于功能族或雅各布阶梯的梯级的简单规则。泛函的特点是动态和光谱特性往往比结构特性更难用 DFT 重现，这对泛函的选择有直接影响。我们的框架还提供了一个通用策略，用于对任何原子模型（包括新一代基础 MLP）进行基准测试，以固液界面的高精度参考为基准。除了模拟之外，这项工作还确定了哪些模型可以信赖，可以将测量到的接触角、摩擦力和振动光谱与其背后的分子结构联系起来。

English Abstract

Simulations of water at solid interfaces routinely disagree on basic properties, from contact angle to friction, depending on the model used, whether an empirical force field or a density functional theory (DFT) exchange-correlation (XC) approximation. Yet no accurate computational reference has been available to determine which, if any, is reliable. Here we assess XC approximations spanning the rungs of Jacob's Ladder against the random-phase approximation (RPA) at the graphene-water interface. Using machine-learned potentials (MLPs) trained on both DFT and RPA data, we achieve the extensive sampling required to characterize interfacial structure, wettability, friction, and vibrational sum-frequency generation spectra. We find that XC approximations disagree substantially with RPA and with each other. To make sense of this, we introduce a transferable, multi-observable scoring framework that distills each functional's agreement with RPA into a single measure. Six functionals emerge as reliable starting points for studies of graphene-water and other dispersion-dominated interfaces, namely B3LYP-D3(0), revPBE-D3(0), revPBE-D3(BJ), B97M-rV, r²SCAN, and revPBE0-D3(0). Notably, this ranking follows no simple rule based on functional family or rung of Jacob's Ladder. What does hold across functionals is that dynamical and spectroscopic properties tend to be harder for DFT to reproduce than structural ones, with direct consequences for functional selection. Our framework also offers a general strategy for benchmarking any atomistic model, including the new generation of foundational MLPs, against high-accuracy references at solid--liquid interfaces. Beyond simulation, this work identifies which models can be trusted to connect measured contact angles, friction, and vibrational spectra to the molecular structure behind them.

使用不变量从粉末 X 射线衍射数据学习晶格参数#

原题：Learning Lattice Parameters from Powder X-Ray Diffraction Data Using Invariants
作者：Elyssa Hofgard、Kyucheol Min、Nofit Segal、David W. Mittan-Moreau、Aria Mansouri Tehrani、Vanessa Oklejas 等 11 人。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-10-06

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们提出了一种机器学习 (ML) 方法，使用新颖的不变晶格表示从粉末 X 射线衍射 (XRD) 数据确定晶胞参数。在机器学习中，所使用的数据表示会对预测质量产生重大影响。以前的方法直接从 XRD 输入预测晶格参数 (a,b,c,α,β,γ)。然而，这些参数很大程度上取决于所使用的晶胞缩减或约定。在这项工作中，我们基于双谱（一种由晶格点的球谐投影构建的描述符）构建了与原始单元约定无关的倒晶格的不变表示。格子双谱的计算是可微分的，我们演示了…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种机器学习 (ML) 方法，使用新颖的不变晶格表示从粉末 X 射线衍射 (XRD) 数据确定晶胞参数。在机器学习中，所使用的数据表示会对预测质量产生重大影响。以前的方法直接从 XRD 输入预测晶格参数 (a,b,c,α,β,γ)。然而，这些参数很大程度上取决于所使用的晶胞缩减或约定。在这项工作中，我们基于双谱（一种由晶格点的球谐投影构建的描述符）构建了与原始单元约定无关的倒晶格的不变表示。格子双谱的计算是可微分的，我们演示了如何使用从最近邻查找初始化的基于梯度的优化来反转它。我们证明，通过固定的 ML 模型架构，使用晶格双谱作为 ML 目标而不是晶胞参数可以带来更准确的晶格参数预测。例如，与使用相同模型架构的直接预测相比，使用 MP-20 数据集，双谱将长度平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 从 11.08% 降低到 2.43%，将角度 MAPE 从 12.15% 降低到 2.45%。我们还根据现有的 XRD 晶体结构模型（例如 Crystalyze）对我们的方法进行了基准测试，并根据实验数据评估其性能。除了晶胞表示之外，我们预计这种不变的晶格表示可以更广泛地作为其他晶体学机器学习任务（例如结构生成）的几何感知目标。

English Abstract

We present a machine learning (ML) method to determine unit cell parameters from powder X-Ray diffraction (XRD) data using a novel invariant lattice representation. In ML, the data representation used can have a substantial impact on the prediction quality. Previous approaches have directly predicted lattice parameters (a,b,c,α,β,γ) from XRD inputs. However, these parameters depend strongly on the unit cell reduction or convention used. In this work, we construct an invariant representation of the reciprocal lattice that is independent of primitive cell convention, based on the bispectrum--a descriptor built from spherical harmonic projections of lattice points. The calculation of the lattice bispectrum is differentiable, and we demonstrate how to invert it using gradient-based optimization initialized from a nearest-neighbor lookup. We show that with a fixed ML model architecture, using the lattice bispectrum as the ML target rather than the unit cell parameters leads to more accurate lattice parameter predictions. For example, using the MP-20 dataset, the bispectrum reduces length mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) from 11.08% to 2.43% and angle MAPE from 12.15% to 2.45% compared to direct prediction with the same model architecture. We additionally benchmark our approach against pre-existing XRD to crystal structure models such as Crystalyze and assess its performance on experimental data. Beyond unit cell representation, we anticipate this invariant lattice representation could serve more broadly as a geometry-aware target for other crystallographic machine learning tasks such as structure generation.

界面能和相变：三元取代合金中基于Cahn-Hilliard和CALPHAD的模型的比较分析#

原题：Interface Energy and Phase Transformations: A Comparative Analysis of Cahn-Hilliard and CALPHAD-based Models in Ternary Substitutional Alloys
作者：Wolfgang Flachberger、Thomas Antretter、Swaroop Gaddikere-Nagaraja、Silvia Leitner、Manuel Petersmann、Jiri Svoboda。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-08

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：合金中的扩散相变通过Cahn-Hilliard类型的相场方法进行建模，其中双阱自由能和梯度项施加界面能，或者通过反应扩散模型进行建模，其中驱动力来自CALPHAD方法，摩尔自由能的凸壳，并且界面不携带能量。它们的数值方案与它们的自由能有关：当界面能量设置为零时，相场方案就会崩溃，而反应扩散码则依赖于有限的差异。我们从热力学极值原理推导出一个涵盖两个极限的单一有限元方案：瑞利函数（自由能率加上耗散的一半，乘法器施加质量守恒）相对于速率、通…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：vacancies。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

合金中的扩散相变通过Cahn-Hilliard类型的相场方法进行建模，其中双阱自由能和梯度项施加界面能，或者通过反应扩散模型进行建模，其中驱动力来自CALPHAD方法，摩尔自由能的凸壳，并且界面不携带能量。它们的数值方案与它们的自由能有关：当界面能量设置为零时，相场方案就会崩溃，而反应扩散码则依赖于有限的差异。我们从热力学极值原理推导出一个涵盖两个极限的单一有限元方案：瑞利函数（自由能率加上耗散的一半，乘法器施加质量守恒）相对于速率、通量和乘法器在冻结状态下的平稳性。通量和化学亲和力是主要的未知数，因此不需要亲和力的衍生物。时间离散方案是相应的增量最小化；它精确地守恒质量，并满足每个凸摩尔自由能的离散能量耗散不等式，包括没有正则化的凸包。我们明确指出，最后一种情况是Stefan类型的退化抛物线问题，其退化可以在不改变自由能的情况下，仅通过移动网格来消除；由于我们的目标是耦合到连续统力学和固定网格上的损伤，因此我们在退化边界上操作，并表明质量守恒、能量平衡和第二定律在那里保持不变。证明了多分量系统的热力学一致性，提出了一种涉及Onsager和扩散系数的方案，并在二元和三元实例中研究了界面能量的作用，后者以空位作为非保守分量。

English Abstract

Diffusional phase transformations in alloys are modeled either by phase-field methods of Cahn-Hilliard type, in which a double-well free energy and a gradient term impose an interface energy, or by reactive diffusion models, in which the driving forces follow from the CALPHAD methodology, the convex hull of the molar free energy, and the interface carries no energy. Their numerical schemes are tied to their free energies: phase-field schemes break down when the interface energy is set to zero, and reactive diffusion codes rely on finite differences. We derive, from the thermodynamic extremal principle, a single finite element scheme that covers both limits: the stationarity of the Rayleighian functional (free energy rate plus one half of the dissipation, with mass conservation imposed by multipliers) with respect to the rates, fluxes and multipliers at a frozen state. Fluxes and chemical affinities are primary unknowns, so no derivative of the affinity is required. The time-discrete scheme is the corresponding incremental minimization; it conserves mass exactly and satisfies a discrete energy-dissipation inequality for every convex molar free energy, including the convex hull without regularization. We make explicit that this last case is a degenerate parabolic problem of Stefan type, whose degeneracy can be removed, without altering the free energy, only by a moving mesh; since our aim is the coupling to continuum mechanics and damage on a fixed mesh, we operate at the boundary of degeneracy and show that mass conservation, the energy balance and the second law remain intact there. Thermodynamic consistency is proved for multi-component systems, a scheme relating Onsager and diffusion coefficients is proposed, and the role of interface energy is studied in binary and ternary examples, the latter with vacancies as a non-conserved component.

热场电子发射计算的高效数值模型#

原题：A numerical and efficient model for thermo-field electron emission calculations
作者：Salvador Barranco Cárceles、Andreas Kyritsakis、Anthony Ayari。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-07

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：随着电子源尺寸的减小、采用新材料制造或提高性能，热场电子发射的分析公式（即 Fowler-Nordheim 和 Murphy-Good 方程及其基于 JWKB 的扩展）越来越多地应用于其衍生条件范围之外。我们提出了 GETELEC-3，这是一种开源代码，用一维与时间无关的薛定谔方程 (1D-TISE) 的直接数值解来替代它们。已经实施了多种求解 1D-TISE 的方法来测试速度、稳健性和解决方案的不确定性。 Noumerov 算法被发现…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

随着电子源尺寸的减小、采用新材料制造或提高性能，热场电子发射的分析公式（即 Fowler-Nordheim 和 Murphy-Good 方程及其基于 JWKB 的扩展）越来越多地应用于其衍生条件范围之外。我们提出了 GETELEC-3，这是一种开源代码，用一维与时间无关的薛定谔方程 (1D-TISE) 的直接数值解来替代它们。已经实施了多种求解 1D-TISE 的方法来测试速度、稳健性和解决方案的不确定性。 Noumerov 算法被发现速度最快，解的不确定性低于 1%，再加上并行推进所有电子能量的优化内核，计算电流密度的成本降低到几毫秒 - 与 JWKB 评估相当。为了进一步提高计算速度，特别是在复杂的障碍中，神经网络已经在半经典参考的对数残差上进行了训练，并且它以一小部分计算成本再现了优于 0.4% 的电流密度。该代码是为金属和半导体开发的，接受任意势和态密度，并配有用户友好的界面和编译的可执行文件，以实现严格的电子发射计算和数据分析的民主化。

English Abstract

As electron sources are reduced in size, fabricated in new materials, or pushed for performance, the analytical formulations for thermo-field electron emission, namely Fowler-Nordheim and Murphy-Good equations and their JWKB-based extensions, are increasingly applied out of the range of conditions for which they were derived. We present GETELEC-3, an open-source code that replaces them with a direct numerical solution of the one-dimensional time-independent Schrödinger equation (1D-TISE). Several methods to solve the 1D-TISE have been implemented to test for speed, robustness, and solution's uncertainty. The Noumerov algorithm is found to be quickest, with the solution uncertainty below 1%, and together with an optimised kernel that advances all electron energies in parallel, the cost of calculating the current density is reduced to a few milliseconds - comparable to the JWKB evaluation. To further boost the calculation speed, especially in complicated barriers, a neural network has been trained on the residual of the logarithm of the semiclassical reference and it reproduces the current density better than 0.4% at a fraction of the computational cost. The code is developed for metals and semiconductors, accepts arbitrary potentials and density of states, and comes with a user-friendly interface and compiled executable to democratise rigorous electron emission calculations and data analysis.

Atom-JEPA：3D 原子系统的联合嵌入预测架构#

原题：Atom-JEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for 3D Atomistic Systems
作者：Kasper Helverskov Petersen、Rasmus Hannibal Tirsgaard、François R J Cornet、Mikkel Jordahn、Mikkel N. Schmidt。arXiv · physics，2026-10-07

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大规模自监督预训练重塑了现代机器学习，极大地提高了语言和视觉模型泛化下游任务的能力。尽管近年来深度学习在原子系统建模方面取得了长足进步，但该领域的自监督预训练尚未实现可比的下游泛化。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了 Atom-JEPA，这是一种自监督预训练框架，受联合嵌入预测架构的启发，通过互补的原子级和子结构级目标，从未标记的 3D 结构中学习潜在表示。我们在大规模分子和晶体数据集上对 Atom-JEPA 进行预训练，并通过对各种下游属性预测任务进行微调来评估其传输性能。 Atom-JEPA在分子ADMET和量子化学性质预测任务上实现了最先进的性能，并且在预测晶体材料的物理性质方面具有很强的竞争力。这些结果证明了潜在空间预测预训练仅支持结构数据的广泛下游泛化的潜力。代码和预训练模型检查点可在 https://github.com/khelverskovp/atom-jepa 上公开获取

English Abstract

Large-scale self-supervised pretraining has reshaped modern machine learning, substantially advancing the ability of language and vision models to generalize across downstream tasks. While deep learning has driven considerable progress in modeling atomistic systems in recent years, self-supervised pretraining in this domain has not yet achieved comparable downstream generalization. To address this, we introduce Atom-JEPA, a self-supervised pretraining framework that learns latent representations from unlabeled 3D structures through complementary atom-level and substructure-level objectives inspired by joint-embedding predictive architectures. We pretrain Atom-JEPA on large-scale molecular and crystalline datasets and evaluate its transfer performance by fine-tuning on a diverse set of downstream property prediction tasks. Atom-JEPA achieves state-of-the-art performance on molecular ADMET and quantum-chemical property prediction tasks, and is highly competitive in predicting the physical properties of crystalline materials. These results demonstrate the potential of latent-space predictive pretraining to support broad downstream generalization from structural data alone. Code and pretrained model checkpoints are publicly available at https://github.com/khelverskovp/atom-jepa
