用于面向 CFD 的晶体生长模拟的自适应 PINN 训练的组件级评估#

原题：Component-Level Evaluation of Adaptive PINN Training for CFD-Oriented Crystal Growth Simulation
作者：Niruta Chapagain、Rohit Raj、Bertwin Kurisinkal Shine、Aditya A S。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-06
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉6方向匹配3

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：物理信息神经网络 (PINN) 训练可最大限度地减少偏微分方程 (PDE)、边界条件和初始条件损失的加权组合。由于自适应方法会在训练期间修改这些权重，因此它们的加权总损失并不总是可以直接比较。我们在热方程基准和简化的 Czochralski 导向热流体问题的匹配设置下比较固定权重 PINN、梯度归一化 PINN (GNPINN) 和基于规则的自适应控制器 (AgenticPINN)。在晶体生长 MLP 实验中，自适应控制将 PDE 残差…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

物理信息神经网络 (PINN) 训练可最大限度地减少偏微分方程 (PDE)、边界条件和初始条件损失的加权组合。由于自适应方法会在训练期间修改这些权重，因此它们的加权总损失并不总是可以直接比较。我们在热方程基准和简化的 Czochralski 导向热流体问题的匹配设置下比较固定权重 PINN、梯度归一化 PINN (GNPINN) 和基于规则的自适应控制器 (AgenticPINN)。在晶体生长 MLP 实验中，自适应控制将 PDE 残差从 10⁻⁵ 减少到 10⁻⁶，而边界条件损失从 10⁻⁵ 增加到 10⁻²。在热方程基准上，GNPINN 实现了最低的相对 L2 场误差 (0.054)，而 AgenticPINN 获得了最小的 PDE 残差，但相对 L2 误差为 1.368。另外，还使用针对修正的 Czochralski CFD 参数扫描的个案保留测试来评估高斯过程替代项。温度扫描的温度场误差约为 6%，而晶体旋转扫描的轴向速度误差约为 42%。这些发现表明，自适应控制可以提高方程满意度，同时削弱其他物理约束。因此，应该使用单独的 PDE、边界条件和解误差指标来评估 PINN 训练，而不是单独使用加权总损失。

English Abstract

Physics-informed neural network (PINN) training minimizes a weighted combination of partial differential equation (PDE), boundary-condition, and initial-condition losses. Because adaptive methods modify these weights during training, their weighted total losses are not always directly comparable. We compare fixed-weight PINN, gradient-normalized PINN (GNPINN), and a rule-based adaptive controller (AgenticPINN) under matched settings on a heat-equation benchmark and a simplified Czochralski-oriented thermal-fluid problem. In the crystal-growth MLP experiment, adaptive control reduced the PDE residual from the order of 10⁻⁵ to 10⁻⁶, while the boundary-condition loss increased from the order of 10⁻⁵ to 10⁻². On the heat-equation benchmark, GNPINN achieved the lowest relative L₂ field error (0.054), whereas AgenticPINN obtained the smallest PDE residual but a relative L₂ error of 1.368. Gaussian-process surrogates were additionally evaluated using case-wise holdout tests on corrected Czochralski CFD parameter sweeps. The temperature-field error for the temperature sweep was approximately 6%, whereas the axial-velocity error for the crystal-rotation sweep was approximately 42%. These findings show that adaptive control can improve equation satisfaction while weakening other physical constraints. PINN training should therefore be evaluated using separate PDE, boundary-condition, and solution-error metrics rather than weighted total loss alone.

原题：Dynamic Kinetic Evaluation Favors Compositionally Diverse Multicomponent Alloy Nanoparticles for Hydrogen Evolution

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

高熵合金 (HEA) 纳米粒子提供化学上多样化的催化位点，但在其巨大的设计空间中确定最佳成分仍然具有挑战性。我们开发了一个框架，结合了机器学习能量预测、热力学蒙特卡罗采样、贝叶斯优化和动力学蒙特卡罗 (kMC) 模拟，以在析氢反应的九元素组成空间中筛选 85 个原子纳米颗粒模型。基于吸附能的静态优化选择了富含 Pt 的低成分组合物，而基于 kMC 的优化将多元素组合物 Au33Co20Cu6Pd26 在评估的候选物中排名最高。循环解析分析进一步表明，在富含 Co/Pd 的环境中氢吸附，通过中间结合位点迁移，以及在结合较弱的含金环境中频繁的海洛夫斯基解吸。这一途径级证据支持多位点反应图，并说明了结合动力学如何补充静态吸附能量筛选。

English Abstract

High-entropy alloy (HEA) nanoparticles offer chemically diverse catalytic sites, but identifying optimal compositions across their large design space remains challenging. We developed a framework combining machine-learning energy prediction, thermodynamic Monte Carlo sampling, Bayesian optimization, and kinetic Monte Carlo (kMC) simulations to screen 85-atom nanoparticle models across a nine-element composition space for the hydrogen evolution reaction. Static optimization based on adsorption energies selected Pt-rich, low-component compositions, whereas kMC-based optimization ranked the multielement composition Au₃₃Co₂₀Cu₆Pd₂₆ highest among the evaluated candidates. Cycle-resolved analysis further showed hydrogen adsorption at Co/Pd-rich environments, migration through intermediate-binding sites, and frequent Heyrovsky desorption at weaker-binding, Au-containing environments. This pathway-level evidence supports a multisite reaction picture and illustrates how incorporating kinetics can complement static adsorption-energy screening.
